Like in earlier proofs, each of these can be written as a sum, with each term involving a geometric difference quantity along with other coefficients that can be controlled using Proposition
3.8
and (
3.14
).
For example, we can treat
58
58

58

On the left-hand side, the two schematic terms have the same algebraic form.

\displaystyle\rho\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{2})-\rho\,\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\check{\mathsf{m}},\check{\mathsf{w}}^{2})

\displaystyle=\rho\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1}-\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{2})+\rho\,\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}},\mathsf{w}^{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\rho\,\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\check{\mathsf{m}},\mathsf{w}^{2}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{2})

\displaystyle=\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho;\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{w}^{2}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{2})\text{.}

For differences involving a vertical derivative, one must also take into account the difference
\mathsf{D}-\check{\mathsf{D}}
of connections; here, the key is to write this in terms of differences of Christoffel symbols and then apply the last identitiy in (
3.15
).
One example of this is the following:

\displaystyle\rho^{2}\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{D}\mathsf{w}^{1})-\rho^{2}\,\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\check{\mathsf{m}},\check{\mathsf{D}}\check{\mathsf{w}}^{1})

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1}-\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{D}\mathsf{w}^{1})+\rho^{2}\,\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}},\mathsf{D}\mathsf{w}^{1})

\displaystyle\qquad+\rho^{2}\,\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\check{\mathsf{m}},\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{w}^{1}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{1}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\rho^{2}\,\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\check{\mathsf{m}},(\mathsf{D}-\check{\mathsf{D}})\check{\mathsf{w}}^{1})

\displaystyle=\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{w}^{1}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{1}))+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))\text{.}

After a diligent analysis of all such difference terms, we obtain

(7.35)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{0}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho;\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}}))_{\bar{a}}+\sum_{\mathsf{v}\in\{\mathsf{w}^{0},\mathsf{w}^{1},\mathsf{w}^{2}\}}[\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{v}-\check{\mathsf{v}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{v}-\check{\mathsf{v}}))]_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

In addition, using Proposition
3.8
and (
3.13
), we can then express, for
k\in\{1,2\}
,

(7.36)

\displaystyle\mathsf{w}^{k}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{k}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{W}^{k}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(1;\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(1;\mathsf{Q})\text{,}

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{w}^{k}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{k})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{D}\mathsf{W}^{k}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(1;\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(1;\mathsf{Q})

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(1;\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(1;\mathsf{D}\mathsf{Q})\text{.}

A similar computation can be done for
\mathsf{w}^{0}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{0}
and
\mathsf{W}^{\star}
, but we also recall (
3.2
) and (
7.36
):

(7.37)

\displaystyle\mathsf{w}^{0}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{0}

\displaystyle=(\mathsf{w}^{\star}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{\star})+\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}},\mathsf{w}^{2})+\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\mathsf{w}^{2}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{2})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{W}^{\star}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(1;\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(1;\mathsf{Q})+\mathscr{O}_{M}(1;\mathsf{W}^{2})\text{,}

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{w}^{0}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{D}\mathsf{W}^{\star}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(1;\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(1;\mathsf{Q})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(1;\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(1;\mathsf{D}\mathsf{Q})+\mathscr{O}_{M-1}(1;\mathsf{W}^{2})+\mathscr{O}_{M}(1;\mathsf{D}\mathsf{W}^{2})\text{.}

Combining (
7.35
)–(
7.37
), we conclude that

(7.38)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{0}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{Q})+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho;\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{D}\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}}))_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{\mathsf{V}\in\{\mathsf{W}^{\star},\mathsf{W}^{1},\mathsf{W}^{2}\}}[\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{V})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{D}\mathsf{V})]_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

Next, for
\mathscr{A}_{1}
, we apply (
2.35
) twice—once with
\bar{\Box}
and once with
\check{\bar{\Box}}
—and subtract the resulting equations in order to obtain (note that
\mathscr{L}_{\rho}
is independent of the metric)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\rho^{2}[\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{D}_{bc}-\check{\mathsf{g}}^{bc}\check{\mathsf{D}}_{bc}]\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}}+\rho^{2}\,[\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathscr{L}_{\rho}\check{\mathsf{w}})-\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\check{\mathsf{m}},\mathscr{L}_{\rho}\check{\mathsf{w}})]_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\rho\,[\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\check{\mathsf{w}})-\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\check{\mathsf{m}},\check{\mathsf{w}})]_{\bar{a}}+\rho^{2}\,[\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathscr{L}_{\rho}\mathsf{m},\check{\mathsf{w}})-\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-1},\mathscr{L}_{\rho}\check{\mathsf{m}},\check{\mathsf{w}})]_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\rho^{2}\,[\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-2},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{m},\check{\mathsf{w}})-\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-2},\check{\mathsf{m}},\check{\mathsf{m}},\check{\mathsf{w}})]_{\bar{a}}\text{,}

where each matching pair of schematic terms has the same algebraic form.
Similar to the previous treatment of
\mathscr{A}_{0}
, each schematic difference on the right-hand side of the above can be expanded and then controlled using Proposition
3.8
and (
3.14
); this then yields

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\rho^{2}[\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{D}_{bc}-\check{\mathsf{g}}^{bc}\check{\mathsf{D}}_{bc}]\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho,\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathscr{L}_{\rho}(\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}}))_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

In addition, we expand the last term of the above using the third identity in (
3.16
):

(7.39)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\rho^{2}[\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{D}_{bc}-\check{\mathsf{g}}^{bc}\check{\mathsf{D}}_{bc}]\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho,\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{W}^{2})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{A}_{1,v}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho,\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{W}^{2})_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

To handle
\mathscr{A}_{1,v}
, we begin by expanding

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1,v}

\displaystyle=-\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}(\mathsf{D}_{bc}-\check{\mathsf{D}}_{bc})\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}}-\rho^{2}(\mathsf{g}^{bc}-\check{\mathsf{g}}^{bc})\check{\mathsf{D}}_{bc}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle=-\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{D}_{b}(\mathsf{D}-\check{\mathsf{D}})_{c}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}}-\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}(\mathsf{D}-\check{\mathsf{D}})_{b}\check{\mathsf{D}}_{c}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}}-\rho^{2}(\mathsf{g}^{bc}-\check{\mathsf{g}}^{bc})\check{\mathsf{D}}_{bc}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

The operator
\mathsf{D}-\check{\mathsf{D}}
can be expressed in terms of differences of Christoffel symbols.
Applying again Proposition
3.8
, (
3.14
), and the last part of (
3.15
), the above becomes

(7.40)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1,v}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathsf{D}_{b}[(\mathsf{\Gamma}-\check{\mathsf{\Gamma}})^{d}_{ca_{j}}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}]+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-4}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathsf{D}_{b}\{\check{\mathsf{g}}^{de}[\mathsf{D}_{c}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{ea_{j}}+\mathsf{D}_{a_{j}}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{ec}-\mathsf{D}_{e}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{ca_{j}}]\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-4}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}[\mathsf{D}_{bc}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{ea_{j}}+\mathsf{D}_{ba_{j}}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{ec}-\mathsf{D}_{be}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{ca_{j}}]\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-4}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

(Notice in the last step, any term having less than two derivatives of
\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}
can be included with the schematic terms.
In addition,
\check{\mathsf{g}}^{de}
can be converted to
\mathsf{g}^{de}
at the cost of an extra term involving
\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}
, which can also be absorbed into the schematic terms.)

Recalling the definitions (
3.11
) and (
3.12
), then (
7.40
) can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1,v}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}[\mathsf{D}_{bc}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{ea_{j}}+\mathsf{D}_{bc}\mathsf{Q}_{a_{j}e}+\mathsf{D}_{b}\mathsf{B}_{a_{j}ec}]\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-4}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathsf{D}_{bc}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}\mathsf{B})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-4}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

Combining (
7.39
) and the above, we conclude that

(7.41)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{1}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathsf{D}_{bc}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}+\mathscr{O}_{M-4}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho,\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}\mathsf{B})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{W}^{2})_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

Now, for the remaining term
\mathscr{A}_{2}
, we begin by expanding

(7.42)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{2}

\displaystyle=-\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}\,\bar{\Box}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}-\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}(\bar{\Box}+\sigma_{\mathsf{w}})\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}\,(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}

\displaystyle\qquad-\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\rho}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}\,\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\rho}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}-\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathsf{D}_{b}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}\,\mathsf{D}_{c}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{A}_{2,1}+\mathscr{A}_{2,2}+\mathscr{A}_{2,3}+\mathscr{A}_{2,4}\text{.}

We now expand each of the terms on the right-hand side of (
7.42
).
First, by Proposition
3.8
,

(7.43)

\mathscr{A}_{2,4}=\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

Next, for
\mathscr{A}_{2,3}
, we recall (
2.20
), along with (
3.11
) and the first part of (
3.16
):

(7.44)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{2,3}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathscr{L}_{\rho}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathscr{L}_{\rho}\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

For
\mathscr{A}_{2,2}
, we begin by applying the wave equation (
3.6
) satisfied by
\mathsf{w}
.
By inspecting the right-hand of the appropriate equation and recalling Proposition
3.8
, we obtain

\displaystyle(\bar{\Box}+\sigma_{\mathsf{w}})\mathsf{w}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{2}\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3};\mathsf{D}\mathsf{w}^{k})+\sum_{k=0}^{2}\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{w}^{k})

\displaystyle=\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2})\text{.}

From the above, we then obtain the following asymptotics:

(7.45)

\mathscr{A}_{2,2}=\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

For
\mathscr{A}_{2,1}
, we decompose
\bar{\Box}
using (
2.35
) and apply Proposition
3.8
:

(7.46)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{2,1}

\displaystyle=-\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}\,\mathsf{D}_{bc}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathscr{L}_{\rho}^{2}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q}))_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho;\mathscr{L}_{\rho}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q}))_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

For the asympototic terms on the right-hand side, we recall (
3.11
) and (
3.16
):

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\rho}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})

\displaystyle=\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho;\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho;\mathsf{Q})\text{,}

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\rho}^{2}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})

\displaystyle=-2\mathsf{W}^{2}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(1;\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(1;\mathsf{Q})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{-1};\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})\text{.}

Thus, combining (
7.46
) and the above yields

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{2,1}

\displaystyle=-\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}\,\mathsf{D}_{bc}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho;\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{W}^{2})_{\bar{a}}\text{,}

Combining (
7.42
)–(
7.45
) and the above, we have that

(7.47)

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{2}

\displaystyle=-\rho^{2}\mathsf{g}^{bc}\mathsf{g}^{de}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\check{\mathsf{w}}_{\bar{a}_{j}[d]}\,\mathsf{D}_{bc}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}+\mathsf{Q})_{a_{j}e}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho;\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{\bar{a}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{D}\mathsf{Q})_{\bar{a}}+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2};\mathsf{W}^{2})_{\bar{a}}\text{.}

Finally, both (
3.18
) and (
3.19
) follow from (
7.34
), (
7.38
), (
7.41
), and (
7.47
).

7.10.
Proof of Proposition
3.15

The first part of (
3.20
) is simply the first equation in (
3.16
), while the second identity in (
3.20
) follows from the final part of (
3.3
), along with Proposition
3.8
and (
3.14
).
(Note this is just the third part of (
3.16
), except we do not renormalize
\mathsf{w}^{2}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{2}
.)

For the remaining identities, we begin by rewriting the first, third, and fourth parts of (
3.5
) as

(7.48)

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\rho}(\rho^{2-n}\mathsf{w}^{2})_{ab}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2-n}\mathsf{g}^{cd}\mathsf{D}_{c}\mathsf{w}^{1}_{bad}+\rho^{2-n}\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-2},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{0})_{ab}+\rho^{2-n}\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{2})_{ab}\text{,}

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\rho}(\rho^{-1}\mathsf{w}^{1})_{abc}

\displaystyle=\rho^{-1}\mathsf{D}_{b}\mathsf{w}^{2}_{ac}-\rho^{-1}\mathsf{D}_{c}\mathsf{w}^{2}_{ab}+\rho^{-1}\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{1})_{abc}\text{,}

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\rho}\mathsf{w}^{0}_{abcd}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{D}_{a}\mathsf{w}^{1}_{bcd}-\mathsf{D}_{b}\mathsf{w}^{1}_{acd}+\rho^{-1}\mathsf{g}_{ad}\mathsf{w}^{2}_{bc}+\rho^{-1}\mathsf{g}_{bc}\mathsf{w}^{2}_{ad}-\rho^{-1}\mathsf{g}_{ac}\mathsf{w}^{2}_{bd}-\rho^{-1}\mathsf{g}_{bd}\mathsf{w}^{2}_{ac}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{0})_{abcd}+\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{2})_{abcd}\text{.}

In particular, here we converted
\bar{\mathsf{D}}_{\rho}
to
\mathscr{L}_{\rho}
using (
2.20
).
We then take the difference of each of the equations in (
7.48
), applied once with respect to
\mathsf{g}
and once with respect to
\check{\mathsf{g}}
, and we treat the resulting difference terms as in the proofs of Propositions
3.13
and
3.14
.
In particular, various geometric quantities can be controlled using Proposition
3.8
and (
3.14
); moreover, the difference
\mathsf{D}-\check{\mathsf{D}}
can be controlled by
\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})
using the last part of (
3.15
).

For example, from the first part of (
7.48
), we have

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\rho}[\rho^{2-n}(\mathsf{w}^{2}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{2})]_{ab}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2-n}(\mathsf{g}^{cd}-\check{\mathsf{g}}^{cd})\mathsf{D}_{c}\mathsf{w}^{1}_{bad}+\rho^{2-n}\check{\mathsf{g}}^{cd}\mathsf{D}_{c}(\mathsf{w}^{1}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{1})_{bad}+\rho^{2-n}\check{\mathsf{g}}^{cd}(\mathsf{D}-\check{\mathsf{D}})_{c}\check{\mathsf{w}}^{1}_{bad}

\displaystyle\qquad+[\rho^{2-n}\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-2},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{0})-\rho^{2-n}\,\mathscr{S}(\check{\mathsf{g}}^{-2},\check{\mathsf{m}},\check{\mathsf{w}}^{0})]_{ab}

\displaystyle\qquad+[\rho^{2-n}\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{2})-\rho^{2-n}\,\mathscr{S}(\mathsf{g}^{-1},\mathsf{m},\mathsf{w}^{2})]_{ab}\text{,}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{O}_{M}(\rho^{2-n};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{w}^{1}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{1}))_{ab}+\mathscr{O}_{M-4}(\rho^{3-n};\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})_{ab}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{2-n};\mathsf{m}-\check{\mathsf{m}})_{ab}+\mathscr{O}_{M-3}(\rho^{3-n};\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}))_{ab}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3-n};\mathsf{w}^{0}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{0})+\mathscr{O}_{M-2}(\rho^{3-n};\mathsf{w}^{2}-\check{\mathsf{w}}^{2})\text{,}

which yields the third equation in (
3.20
).
Note that in the last step, we used the improved vanishing rate for
\mathsf{w}^{1}
in (
3.9
) when dealing with terms involving
\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}}
and
\mathsf{D}(\mathsf{g}-\check{\mathsf{g}})
.
The remaining parts of (
3.20
) are obtained analogously, once again using the improved rates for
\mathsf{w}^{1}
and
\mathsf{w}^{2}
in (
3.9
).
